Let's define function `initial_shape(N, A, gamma, two=False, rng=None)`:
- If two: m=N//2; a zeros; a[:m]=2/(3*m); a[m:]=1/(3*(N-m)) (if N odd?). Normalize.
- Power: m=N//2; left indices 0..m-1: t=(i+1?)/(m)? Need edge zero derivative? Use t=i/m (0 for first). But first a=1, okay. However if a[0]=1, c0 small for large N. Right: u=(m-(s+1?))/m? To make last edge 1, use t=(m - s -1)/m? For s=0 (adjacent center), t=(m-1)/m ~1 -> 1-A; for s=m-1, t=0 ->1. left s=0 edge t=0->1, last left t=(m-1)/m ~1 ->1-A. Good.
  left = 1 + A*(i/m)^γ; right = 1 - A*((m-1-s)/m)^γ. Sum not exactly N if left sum A∑(i/m)^γ, right -A∑((m-1-s)/m)^γ = same, sum N. Good. Normalize by N (so average 1). This has sum1.
- Random anti: base 1 + noise anti: generate r left length m; a left =1+A*r/m? right=1-A*r_reversed/m? Normalize, clip min0.
- Previous best: take best sequence, downsample to N by averaging nonoverlap blocks, normalize, then upsample.

Run L-BFGS:
We need functions use global `CUR_N`, `CUR_BETA`, maybe `STOP_FLAG`.
Top-level:
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

CUR_N=0
CUR_BETA=1.0
CUR_L=0
STOP=False

def softmax_tail(t):
    z0 = np.empty(CUR_N)
    z0[0]=0; z0[1:]=t
    z = z0 - z0.max()
    p = np.exp(z)
    p /= p.sum()
    return p
```
But if z0 max at 0 fixed? We set z0[0]=0; subtract max may make z0 negative, fine. This parameterization has a0 not necessarily largest; allows all. If max at other, a0 small. Good.

L choice: next power of two >=3N-2. Precompute lib.
```
def fft_score_grad(t, beta):
   a=softmax_tail(t)
   Af=np.fft.rfft(a, CUR_L)
   C=np.fft.irfft(Af*Af, CUR_L)[:2*CUR_N-1]
   cm=C.max()
   sh=beta*(C-cm)
   sh=np.clip(sh,-100,0)
   w=np.exp(sh); W=w.sum()
   g=cm+np.log(W)/beta
   f=2*CUR_N*g
   Wf=np.fft.rfft(w,CUR_L) # w length 2N-1, but passing 1D length 2N-1 to rfft ok zero pads to CUR_L
   cross=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Wf)*Af,CUR_L)
   v=cross[:CUR_N]
   grad_a=2.0*v
   dot=np.dot(a,grad_a)
   grad_z=a*(grad_a-dot)
   # tail gradient is grad_z[1:]
   return f, grad_z[1:]
```
Need ensure Wf convolution with Af: cross formula earlier: ifft(conj(fft(w))*fft(a)) gives (w ⋆ a)[j]=∑ w[k] a[k-j]. Good.
But w length 2N-1, Af length L. Need rfft(w, L) zero pads. OK.

Check gradient scaling: f=2N*g, g=logsumexp(beta c)/beta. ∂g/∂a = ∑ w ∂c/∂a. w normalized. So grad_a =2 cross. Good.
Potential issue: C computed via circular convolution length L; for L>=2N-1 first 2N-1 are linear. Good.

Scipy minimize:
```
def minimize_tail(t, beta, maxiter, time_limit):

Let's design exact score function:
```
def norm_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n==0: return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.inf
    a = a/s
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    Af=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Af*Af,L)[:2*n-1]
    return 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
```
This score ignores clip upper and exact sum? Equivalent if nonneg and <=1000. `evaluate_sequence` uses np.convolve direct, but FFT tiny roundoff. For official best, use `evaluate_sequence` when n manageable. But if we return optimized prototype, final evaluator will call exact. We can use `evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())` for updating best to ensure valid.

Next power of two helper:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return p
```

Resampling best to N:
```
def resample_seq(a, N):
    b=np.asarray(a)
    n=len(b)
    if n==1: return np.full(N,b[0])
    out=np.zeros(N)
    # average blocks mapping from target to source
    for i in range(N): out[i]=b[int(i*n/N)]
    # simpler: weights
```

Initial shape function top-level:
```
def make_power_seq(N, A, gamma, rng=None, seed=0):
    a=np.ones(N)
    m=N//2
    if m==0: return a
    i=np.arange(m, dtype=float)
    left_a = 1.0 + A * np.power(i/m, gamma)  # i=0 edge 1, i=m-1 near 1-A
    s=np.arange(m)
    right_t = (m-1-s)/m
    right_a = 1.0 - A * np.power(right_t, gamma)
    a[:m]=left_a; a[m:]=right_a
    # If N odd, middle? For odd, middle index? anti not possible; set middle=1.0
    if N%2==1:
       # put middle at 1 maybe, adjust? Actually N=2m+1, center index m. For anti, a[m] should be? We can set 1.
       a[m]=1.0
    if rng is not None: add small noise? maybe
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum(); if s>0:a/=s
    return a
```
For odd, left length m, right length m, middle 1. Sum: left sum m + A∑(i/m)^γ, right m - A∑same, middle 1 -> N exactly? Left+right =2m = N-1, middle 1 => N. Good.

Two-block function:
```
def make_two_block(N, ratio=2.0): # left sum ratio/(ratio+1)
   s=ratio/(ratio+1)
   m=N//2
   a=np.zeros(N)
   if m<=0: return np.ones(N)/N
   a[:m]=s/m
   a[m:]=(1-s)/(N-m)
   return a
```
For N=2, A=2/3 values [0.666,0.333] if m=1: left sum 2/3, right 1/3. Good. For large, left height ~ 2/(3m).

def make_random_anti(N, A, rng, smooth=None):
   m=N//2
   r=rng.random(m)
   # optionally cumulative? anti should have high near center; maybe multiply by ramp
   ramp=np.linspace(0.2,1.0,m)
   r=r*(0.5+0.5*ramp)
   maxr=r.max() or1; r=A*r/maxr
   a=np.ones(N); a[:m]=1+r; a[m:]=1-r[::-1]; if odd a[m]=1; normalize
```

But nested? top-level.
```
def smooth_momentum(t, beta, lr, iters):
   global CUR_N, CUR_BETA, CUR_L
   x=t.copy(); v=np.zeros_like(x); best_f=np.inf
   for it in range(iters):
      f,g= _softmax_score_grad(x,beta)
      v=0.9*v - lr*g
      x_new=np.clip(x+v,-25,25)
      f2,_= _softmax_score_grad(x_new,beta)
      if f2 < f:
         x=x_new; v=0.9*v
      else:
         lr*=0.5; v*=0.5
      if f < best_f: best_f=f; best_x=x.copy()
   return best_x
```
No closures. This is coarse.

Top-level helpers:
```
def soft_score_from_a(a, beta):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.inf
    a=a/s
    n=len(a)
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    Af=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Af*Af,L)[:2*n-1]
    cm=c.max()
    sh=np.clip(beta*(c-cm), -100,0)
    w=np.exp(sh)
    W=w.sum()
    return 2*n*(cm+np.log(W)/beta)
```
This computes same f. For exact official fast score use beta large? We can use `2*n*max(c)`.

def optimize_tail_from_start(a, cur_N, deadline):
    global CUR_N,CUR_L,CUR_BETA
    CUR_N=cur_N; CUR_L=next_pow2(3*cur_N-2)
    t=seq_to_z_tail(a)
    t=np.clip(t, -20,20)
    best_t=t; best_f=np.inf
    # maybe compute fast score
    for beta,maxiter in [(5,30),(20,50),(60,80),(200,100)]:
        CUR_BETA=beta
        try:
           res=minimize(fft_score_grad, t, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=TB, options=...)
           if res.x finite and time... t=res.x
        except Exception: pass
        # compute fast score and keep t if better
    return t_to_seq(t)
```

Let's implement robust:
```
LAST_F=None; LAST_G=None; LAST_X=None
def _score_grad_compute(t): ... returns f,g
def fft_fun(t):
    f,g=_score_grad_compute(t)
    global LAST_FUN; LAST_FUN=f
    return f
def fft_grad(t):
    f,g=_score_grad_compute(t)
    return g
```

def tail_to_seq(t):

Let's implement `anti_score_grad(d, beta)` global ANT_N, ANT_L, ANT_BETA.
```
def anti_a(d):
   n=ANT_N; m=n//2; base=1.0/n
   a=np.empty(n)
   a[:m] = base + d
   a[m:] = base - d[::-1] # for N even; if odd middle base
   return a
```
For odd, m=floor(n/2), middle base, right length m; sum base*n + sum d - sum d =1. Use even for simplicity.
Gradient:
- Compute a, Af, C, w, cross, grad_a=2*cross[:n].
- f=2n*g.
- g_left = grad_a[:m] - grad_a[n-1:0:-1]? Right reversed order: for d_j, right idx = n-1-j. The array `grad_a[::-1][:m]` gives grad_a[n-1-j] for j=0..m-1. So grad_d = grad_a[:m] - grad_a[::-1][:m].
- Return f, grad_d.

Bounds: Since nonnegative, d_min=-(1-1e-9)/N, d_max=(1-1e-9)/N. If A=1 exactly, one side zero; allowed. Use 1e-12 margin.

This anti optimizer likely more efficient. Let's make it primary.

def run_anti_optimizer(d0, specs):
    d=np.asarray(d0)
    best_d=d.copy(); best_f=anti_f_only(d)
    for beta,maxiter in specs:
       if time.monotonic() > GLOBAL_DEADLINE-5: break
        ANT_BETA=beta
       try res=minimize(anti_fun, d, jac=anti_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=ANT_BOUNDS, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':20})
       except Exception: break
       d=res.x
       f=fast_score(anti_a(d))
       if f<best_f: best_d=d.copy(); best_f=f
    return best_d, best_f
```

`anti_compute(d)` returns f,grad_d. It uses `_conv_soft_grad`? We can duplicate for anti, or write generic `_conv_soft_grad_from_a(a, beta)` returning f, grad_a, c. Top-level.

Generic:
```
def conv_soft_grad(a, beta, L):
   n=len(a)
   Af=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   C=np.fft.irfft(Af*Af,L)[:2*n-1]
   cm=float(np.max(C))
   sh=np.clip(beta*(C-cm), -100, 0)
   w=np.exp(sh); W=w.sum()
   f=2*n*(cm + np.log(W)/beta)
   Wf=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
   cross=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Wf)*Af,L)
   grad_a=2.0*cross[:n]
   return f, grad_a
```
Then anti_compute calls with L=ANT_L. tail_compute similar but softmax first.

For basis no gradient, use `soft_score_from_a`.

Fast exact score:
```
def fast_score_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<... return inf
    a=a/s; n=len(a); L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    Af=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=np.fft.irfft(Af*Af,L)[:2*n-1]
    return 2*n*float(np.max(c))
```

def anti_momentum(d, beta, lr, iters):
   d=d.copy(); v=np.zeros_like(d); best_f=fast_score_seq(anti_a(d)); best=d.copy()
   for it in range(iters):
      f,g=anti_compute(d)
      v=0.9*v - lr*g
      d_new=d+v
      # project bounds
      lo,hi=ANT_BOUNDS[0]
      d_new=np.clip(d_new,lo,hi)
      f2=fast_score_seq(anti_a(d_new))  # exact no beta maybe costly; use anti_f for speed? Use anti_compute f? f2 from anti_compute d_new with same beta to align
      if f2 < f:
         d=d_new; v=0.9*v
      else:
         lr*=0.5; v*=0.5; break?
   return d,best
```

```
def set_tail_globals(N): global CUR_N,CUR_L,TAI_BOUNDS; ... lim=25; TAI_BOUNDS=[(-lim,lim)]*(N-1)
def tail_compute(t):
   a=tail_to_seq(t)
   f,g_a=conv_soft_grad(a,CUR_BETA,CUR_L)
   dot=np.dot(a,g_a)
   g_z=a*(g_a-dot)
   return f, g_z[1:]
def tail_fun(t): return tail_compute(t)[0]
def tail_grad(t): return tail_compute(t)[1]
```

Let's write start generation for anti:
```
def make_power_d(N, A, gamma):
   m=N//2
   if m==0: return np.zeros(0)
   t=np.arange(m,dtype=float)/m
   d=(A/N)*np.power(t, gamma)
   return d
def make_two_block_d(N, A=1/3): return np.full(N//2, A/N)
def make_random_d(N,A,rng):
   m=N//2
   p=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   r=rng.random(m)
   r = 0.5 + 0.5*r  # positive
   # smooth? cumulative average
   cum=np.cumsum(r); cum/=cum[-1] maybe
   # shape high near center: r*p? Actually t^1.
   r = r * np.power(np.linspace(0,1,m), 0.5)
   r /= r.max()
   return (A/N)*r
def make_chirp_d(N,A):
   m=N//2; x=np.arange(m)/m
   h=np.cos(np.pi*x*x)
   h -= h[0]
   # ensure high near center? multiply by x? 
   r=x*h? ; r /= max; maybe clip positive.
   return (A/N)*r
```

`run_anti_optimizer`:
```
def run_anti_optimizer(u0, specs):
    u=np.asarray(u0,float).copy()
    # project bounds
    if ANT_M>0:
       u=np.clip(u, ANT_BOUNDS[0][0], ANT_BOUNDS[0][1])
    best_u=u.copy()
    best_score=fast_score_seq(anti_a(u))
    for beta,maxiter in specs:
       if time.monotonic() > GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 10: break
       ANT_BETA=float(beta)
       try:
          res=minimize(anti_fun, u, jac=anti_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=ANT_BOUNDS, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'maxfun':max(maxiter*20,200)})
       except Exception:
          break
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          u=np.clip(res.x, ANT_BOUNDS[0][0], ANT_BOUNDS[0][1])
       s=fast_score_seq(anti_a(u))
       if s<best_score:
          best_score=s; best_u=u.copy()
    return best_u, best_score
```
`ANTI_BOUNDS[0]` tuple.

`anti_fun(d)` reads `ANT_BETA` global; but scaling. It calls `anti_compute` which returns scaled. Good.

`fast_score_seq` uses FFT max. For N=2048, each call FFT L=4096? For score compute convolution only L>=2N-1 not 3N; we can use next_pow2(2n-1). faster. For gradient need 3N. OK.

def run_tail_optimizer(a0, specs):
  set_tail_globals(len(a0))

  best_a=a0; best_score=fast_score_seq(a0)
  for beta,maxiter in specs:
     CUR_BETA=beta
     res=minimize(tail_fun, t, jac=tail_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=TAIL_BOUNDS, options...)
     t=clip; a=tail_to_seq(t); s=fast_score_seq(a); update
  return best_a,best_score
```

`anti_compute(u)`:
```
n=ANT_N; m=ANT_M
if time... raise
a=np.empty(n)
a[:m]=ANT_BASE*(1.0+u)
if n%2==0:
   a[m:]=ANT_BASE*(1.0-u[::-1])
else:
   a[m]=ANT_BASE
   a[m+1:]=ANT_BASE*(1.0-u[::-1])
# clip tiny negatives
a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
f,ga=conv_soft_grad(a, ANT_BETA, ANT_L)
if n%2==0:
   grad= (ga[:m] - ga[::-1][:m]) * ANT_BASE
else:
   grad= (ga[:m] - ga[n-1:n-m:-1]? need right order) * base
```
For odd, right indices for u_j (j=0..m-1) are n-1-j. `ga[n-1:n-m-1:-1]` slice from n-1 down to m+1 inclusive length m. Or simply `ga[::-1][:m]` still works for odd? If n=5, ga[::-1] indices 4,3,2,1,0. right for u0 is4, u1=3; [:m]=4,3 correct. The middle index 2 not included. Good. For even n=4, ga[::-1] indices3,2,1,0; right for u0=3,u1=2; correct. So use `ga[::-1][:m]`.
Scale = float(n)*float(n); return f*scale, grad*scale.

`anti_fun(u)`: return anti_compute(u)[0]; `anti_grad`: [1].

`tail_to_seq(t)`: If CUR_N<=1 return ones. z[0]=0,z[1:]=t; z-=max; p=exp; p/=sum; return p.

`tail_compute(t)`:
```
a=tail_to_seq(t)
f,ga=conv_soft_grad(a,CUR_BETA,CUR_L)
dot=float(np.dot(a,ga))
gz=a*(ga-dot)
scale=float(CUR_N)*float(CUR_N)
return f*scale, gz[1:]*scale
```

Make u starts:
```
def make_power_u(N,A,gamma):
   m=N//2
   if m==0: return np.zeros(0)
   t=np.arange(m,dtype=float)/m
   u=A*np.power(t,gamma)
   return np.clip(u,-1,1)
def make_two_block_u(N,A=1.0/3.0): return np.full(N//2,A)
def make_poly_u(N,c0,c1,A=0.9):
   m=N//2; t=np.arange(m)/m; u=c0*t + c1*t*t; if max>1 u*=A/max; return clip
def make_random_u(N,A,rng):
   m=N//2; u=A*rng.normal(0,1,m); # smooth? u=np.cumsum(u); u-=u.mean(); norm
   # To keep bounded and shape, use random then cumulative with endpoints zero:
   r=rng.normal(0,1,m+1); r[0]=0; r=-np.cumsum(r); r[-1]=0? too complex.
   u = A * np.clip(u, -1, 1)
   return u
```
Random signed may have high frequency; could be useful. Smooth random: generate few Fourier amplitudes? Simpler: `u = A*(np.sin(2π*rng... *t + phase))` with anti? Could be. We'll include one sinusoidal: `u=A*np.sin(2*np.pi*(rng.uniform(0.5,3)*t - 0.25))` clipped. But optimal profile likely monotone; random signed not necessary. Use `make_random_u` as `A*np.tanh(rng.normal(0,1,m))` then smooth by cumulative moving average? Maybe skip.

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1800,**kwargs):
   global GLOBAL_DEADLINE, ...
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   GLOBAL_DEADLINE=time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s)-10
   best_a=None; best_score=np.inf
   # init with height_sequence_1
   try:
      a0=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float); a0=np.clip(a0,0,1000); s=a0.sum(); if s>0: a0/=s; score=fast_score_seq(a0); update
      if len(a0)>0 and time... official maybe not.
   except: pass
   # fallbacks
   for seq in [np.array([2/3,1/3]), np.array([1.6,0.8,0.6])/3]: update fast
   # maybe power length 128 initial
   for N in [64,128]: u=make_power_u(N,0.9,2.0); set_anti_globals(N); a=anti_a(u); update
   if best_score < 1.45? continue anyway
   prev_u=None; prev_N=0
   for N in [32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048]:
       remaining=GLOBAL_DEADLINE-time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 60: break

       set_anti_globals(N)

           a=anti_a(u); sc=fast_score_seq(a); if sc<best_score: update best_a, best_score; prev_u=u; prev_N=N
           print...
       if best_score < 1.45 and N>=512: maybe continue? no.
   # tail backup if time and best_score > 1.5027 maybe

   # final official check

   best_a=np.clip(np.asarray(best_a,float),0,1000); s=best_a.sum(); if s>0: best_a/=s
   try: official=official_score(best_a)
   except: official=best_score
   if not finite official: official=best_score
   # Maybe official of fallback [2/3,1/3] if best official worse
   # Return list

For N=2 to4, constant start okay.

In `propose_candidate`, after anti loop, if `prev_u` updated, but best_a maybe longer? We update best_a when score improves. `prev_u` should be associated with best local N, not necessarily global best. For resampling to next N, using local best (best_u at that N) is good even if global best different length. But if a different start at smaller N later improved global, prev_u may not be global. We can keep `current_u` local and `best_u_global`? Simpler: At each N, use starts including resample of **best anti found so far globally**? Store `best_u_global` and `best_N_global`. When updating best_a, if sequence came from anti, store u and N. But after tail, best_a may not be anti. For next anti N, we can resample from `best_u_global` if exists (anti profile) or use power starts. If best is tail non-anti, can't u-resample; but anti starts still include powers. To use best anti, store separately.

Implement:
- `best_anti_u=None; best_anti_N=0`.
- When anti score improves global best or local? We can update best_anti_u if score improves `best_anti_score` (not necessarily global). This gives best shape across N. Use it for resampling.
Simpler: within N, after refine, if local score < `best_anti_score`, update best_anti_u/N. The local score likely <= global best. Use that for next N. Good.
Initialize best_anti_score=inf.

In N loop:
```
refined_u, refined_s = ...
if refined_s < best_anti_score:
    best_anti_u=refined_u; best_anti_N=N; best_anti_score=refined_s
    if refined_s < best_score: update best_a
# For next starts, if best_anti_u and best_anti_N<N: resample best_anti_u
```
Thus prev local is best anti.

Need if best_anti_u length m_old; resample to new N.

For tail backup, if best_a None etc.

`make_general_random_seq(N, rng)`:
```
a=np.ones(N)+0.5*rng.normal(0,1,N); a=np.clip(a,0,None); normalize
```

Could also anti start from `best_anti_u` resampled.

Now code details.

Let's produce final. Ensure `print` not too many; per N.

One possible issue: L-BFGS-B with bounds and analytic gradient scaled by N²; for N=2048 f~4e6, ftol 1e-12 absolute too strict, but maxiter. `gtol=1e-7` maybe if gradient small. OK.

If `minimize` raises due to `maxfun` limit, res still available; catch `Exception` and maybe use res? In except, we don't have res. Could catch and continue. Fine.

Use `np.isfinite(res.x).all()`.

Time exception: If TimeUp raised inside fun, catch in `run_anti_optimizer` with `except TimeUp: break` and `except Exception: break`. But `minimize` may nested; okay.

Define TimeUp top-level.

In compute functions, check deadline only if `GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0`. If raised, best_u maybe from previous stage.

Let's write final code now.
</think>

```python
\"\"\"
Anti-palindromic multi-resolution search.

The objective is scale invariant and can be written as
    2 * n * max_k conv(a, a)_k / sum(a)^2.
A very useful family of candidates is anti-palindromic around the center:
    a[j] = (1/N) * (1 + u[j])          for j < N/2
    a[N-1-j] = (1/N) * (1 - u[j])      for j >= N/2
with |u[j]| <= 1.  This guarantees non-negativity and exact normalization.

For each length N we optimize the vector u with L-BFGS-B on a smooth
log-sum-exp surrogate of the max-convolution term.  The gradient is computed
by FFT:
    d conv(a,a)/d a = 2 * cross-convolution(weights, a).

The solver is run in an increasing sequence of lengths.  The best anti-
palindromic profile found at a coarser scale is linearly upsampled and used
as a starting point at a finer scale.  A general (non-anti) softmax search is
used as a fallback polish when time remains.
\"\"\"

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0

ANT_N = 0
ANT_M = 0
ANT_BASE = 0.0
ANT_L = 1
ANT_BETA = 1.0
ANT_BOUNDS = []

CUR_N = 0
CUR_L = 1
CUR_BETA = 1.0
CUR_BOUNDS = []


class TimeUp(Exception):
    pass


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p *= 2
    return p


def _fast_score_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    n = int(a.size)

    if n <= 256:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
        m = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        Af = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(Af * Af, L)[:2 * n - 1]
        m = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _official_score(a):
    return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in np.asarray(a).tolist()]))


def _conv_soft_grad(a, beta, L):
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    n = int(a.size)

    Af = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Af * Af, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))

    shifted = np.clip(beta * (c - cmax), -100.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(shifted)
    W = float(w.sum())
    if W <= 0.0:
        W = 1.0

    g = cmax + np.log(W) / beta
    f = float(2.0 * n * g)

    Wf = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    cross = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Wf) * Af, L)
    grad_a = 2.0 * cross[:n]
    return f, grad_a


def _set_ant_globals(N):
    global ANT_N, ANT_M, ANT_BASE, ANT_L, ANT_BOUNDS
    ANT_N = int(N)
    ANT_M = int(ANT_N // 2)
    ANT_BASE = 1.0 / float(ANT_N)
    ANT_L = _next_pow2(3 * ANT_N - 2)
    lim = 1.0 - 1e-10
    if ANT_M > 0:
        ANT_BOUNDS = [(-lim, lim)] * ANT_M
    else:
        ANT_BOUNDS = []


def _anti_a(u):
    n = ANT_N
    m = ANT_M
    base = ANT_BASE
    u = np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64), -1.0 + 1e-10, 1.0 - 1e-10)

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m > 0:
        a[:m] = base * (1.0 + u)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        if m > 0:
            a[m:] = base * (1.0 - u[::-1])
    else:
        if m > 0:
            a[m] = base
            a[m + 1:] = base * (1.0 - u[::-1])
        else:
            a[0] = base
    return np.maximum(a, 0.0)


def _ant_compute(u):
    if GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.monotonic() > GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise TimeUp
    n = ANT_N
    m = ANT_M
    if m <= 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0)

    u = np.clip(np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64), -1.0 + 1e-10, 1.0 - 1e-10)
    a = _anti_a(u)
    f, ga = _conv_soft_grad(a, ANT_BETA, ANT_L)

    # d a / d u has base in the first half and -base in the reflected second half.
    grad_u = ANT_BASE * (ga[:m] - ga[::-1][:m])
    scale = float(n) * float(n)
    return float(f) * scale, grad_u * scale


def _ant_fun(u):
    val, _ = _ant_compute(u)
    return val


def _ant_grad(u):
    _, gr = _ant_compute(u)
    return gr


def _ant_optimize(u0, specs):
    if ANT_M <= 0:
        return np.copy(u0), _fast_score_seq(_anti_a(u0))

    lo, hi = ANT_BOUNDS[0]
    u = np.clip(np.asarray(u0, dtype=np.float64), lo, hi).copy()
    best_u = u.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score_seq(_anti_a(u))

    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        # Simple projected momentum fallback.
        momentum = np.zeros_like(u)
        lr = 0.25
        beta = 50.0
        iters = 120
        ANT_BETA = beta
        for _ in range(iters):
            if GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.monotonic() > GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
                break
            f, g = _ant_compute(u)
            momentum = 0.9 * momentum - lr * (g / (float(ANT_N) ** 2))
            u_new = np.clip(u + momentum, lo, hi)
            f2, _ = _ant_compute(u_new)
            if f2 < f:
                u = u_new
                momentum = 0.9 * momentum
            else:
                lr *= 0.5
                momentum *= 0.5
            sc = _fast_score_seq(_anti_a(u))
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_u = u.copy()
        return best_u, best_score

    for beta, maxiter in specs:
        if GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.monotonic() > GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0:
            break
        ANT_BETA = float(beta)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _ant_fun,
                u,
                jac=_ant_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=ANT_BOUNDS,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "maxfun": int(max(200, 8 * maxiter)),
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-7,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
        except TimeUp:
            break
        except Exception:
            continue

        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            u = np.clip(res.x, lo, hi)

        sc = _fast_score_seq(_anti_a(u))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_u = u.copy()

    return best_u, best_score


def _make_power_u(N, A=0.9, gamma=2.0):
    m = N // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    if m == 1:
        t = np.array([1.0])
    else:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    u = A * np.power(t, gamma)
    return np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)


def _make_two_block_u(N, A=1.0 / 3.0):
    return np.full(max(1, N // 2), A, dtype=np.float64)


def _make_poly_u(N, c0, c1, A=0.95):
    m = N // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    if m == 1:
        t = np.array([1.0])
    else:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    u = c0 * t + c1 * np.power(t, 2.0)
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(u)))
    if mx > 1e-12:
        u = A * u / mx
    return np.clip(u, -1.0, 1.0)


def _make_rand_u(N, A=0.9, rng=None):
    m = N // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    if m == 1:
        u = np.array([A * 0.5])
    else:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        a1 = rng.uniform(0.2, 2.0)
        a2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        u = A * np.tanh(0.6 * t + 0.8 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * a1 * t + ph) + 0.4 * a2 * t ** 3)
    return np.clip(u, -1.0 + 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _resample_u(u_old, N_old, N_new):
    m_old = int(N_old) // 2
    m_new = int(N_new) // 2
    if m_old <= 0 or m_new <= 0:
        return np.zeros(m_new)
    u_old = np.asarray(u_old, dtype=np.float64)
    if m_old == 1:
        out = np.full(m_new, float(u_old[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        t_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m_old)
        t_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m_new)
        out = np.interp(t_new, t_old, u_old)
    return np.clip(out, -1.0 + 1e-10, 1.0 - 1e-10)


def _set_cur_globals(N):
    global CUR_N, CUR_L, CUR_BOUNDS
    CUR_N = int(N)
    CUR_L = _next_pow2(3 * CUR_N - 2)
    lim = 25.0
    if CUR_N > 1:
        CUR_BOUNDS = [(-lim, lim)] * (CUR_N - 1)
    else:
        CUR_BOUNDS = []


def _tail_to_seq(t):
    n = CUR_N
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1)

    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[0] = 0.0
    z[1:] = np.clip(np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64), -25.0, 25.0)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(n)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _seq_to_tail_z(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(0)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-300, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n - 1)
    z = np.log(a / s)
    t = z[1:] - z[0]
    return np.clip(t, -25.0, 25.0)


def _cur_compute(t):
    if GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.monotonic() > GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise TimeUp
    n = CUR_N
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0)

    a = _tail_to_seq(t)
    f, ga = _conv_soft_grad(a, CUR_BETA, CUR_L)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gz = a * (ga - dot)
    scale = float(n) * float(n)
    return float(f) * scale, gz[1:] * scale


def _cur_fun(t):
    val, _ = _cur_compute(t)
    return val


def _cur_grad(t):
    _, gr = _cur_compute(t)
    return gr


def _cur_optimize(a0, specs):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a0.copy(), _fast_score_seq(a0)

    _set_cur_globals(n)
    t = _seq_to_tail_z(a0)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score_seq(a0)

    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best_a, best_score

    lo, hi = -25.0, 25.0
    for beta, maxiter in specs:
        if GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.monotonic() > GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 5.0:
            break
        CUR_BETA = float(beta)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _cur_fun,
                t,
                jac=_cur_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=CUR_BOUNDS,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "maxfun": int(max(120, 6 * maxiter)),
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-7,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
        except TimeUp:
            break
        except Exception:
            continue

        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            t = np.clip(res.x, lo, hi)

        a = _tail_to_seq(t)
        sc = _fast_score_seq(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    \"\"\"Search for a short, good sequence and return it as a Python list.\"\"\"
    global GLOBAL_DEADLINE

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    GLOBAL_DEADLINE = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s)) - 10.0

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    def _consider(a, tag=""):
        nonlocal best_a, best_score
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            return
        a = a / s
        sc = _fast_score_seq(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
            print(f"[init] {tag}: score={sc:.10f} len={a.size}")

    # Initialize from the supplied construction, if available.
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals() and height_sequence_1 is not None:
            _consider(np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64), "provided")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Strong small fallbacks.
    _consider(np.array([2.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0]), "2-point")
    _consider(np.array([1.6, 0.8, 0.6]) / 3.0, "3-block")

    # Multi-resolution anti-palindromic search.
    best_ant_u = None
    best_ant_N = 0
    best_ant_score = np.inf

    lengths = [8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048]
    for N in lengths:
        remaining = GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 60.0:
            break
        if N >= 2048 and remaining < 420.0:
            break
        if N >= 1024 and remaining < 240.0:
            break
        if N >= 512 and remaining < 150.0:
            break

        _set_ant_globals(N)

        starts = []

        if best_ant_u is not None and best_ant_N != N:
            starts.append(_resample_u(best_ant_u, best_ant_N, N))

        starts.append(_make_power_u(N, 0.95, 2.0))
        starts.append(_make_power_u(N, 1.0, 1.6))
        starts.append(_make_two_block_u(N, 0.34))

        if N <= 128:
            starts.append(_make_poly_u(N, 0.35, 0.65, 0.95))
            starts.append(_make_rand_u(N, 0.9, rng))

        local_best_u = None
        local_best_score = np.inf

        screen_specs = [(20, 50)] if N <= 128 else [(30, 70)]
        for u0 in starts:
            if GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() < 25.0:
                break
            u, sc = _ant_optimize(u0, screen_specs)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_score:
                local_best_score = sc
                local_best_u = u

        if local_best_u is not None:
            if N <= 128:
                refine_specs = [(60, 80), (150, 120), (350, 160)]
            elif N <= 512:
                refine_specs = [(80, 100), (220, 140)]
            else:
                refine_specs = [(100, 120), (300, 160)]

            u, sc = _ant_optimize(local_best_u, refine_specs)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_ant_score:
                    best_ant_score = sc
                    best_ant_u = u.copy()
                    best_ant_N = N
                if sc <= local_best_score:
                    a = _anti_a(u)
                    _consider(a, f"anti N={N}")
                print(f"[anti] N={N} refined score={sc:.10f} best_score={best_score:.10f}")

    # General softmax polish, if useful.
    if HAVE_SCIPY and best_a is not None and GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() > 150.0:
        # Use a manageable length for the general search.
        if best_a.size <= 512:
            n_tail = max(32, int(best_a.size))
        else:
            n_tail = 256
            x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, best_a.size)
            x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_tail)
            init_tail = np.interp(x_new, x_old, best_a)
        if best_a.size <= 512:
            init_tail = best_a.astype(np.float64).copy()

        # A pure anti initial point for general search is good, but also try a random one.
        inits = [init_tail]
        if n_tail >= 32:
            rr = np.ones(n_tail, dtype=np.float64)
            rr += 0.45 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n_tail)
            rr = np.clip(rr, 0.0, None)
            if rr.sum() > 0:
                inits.append(rr / rr.sum())

        general_specs = [(20, 50), (100, 80)]
        for init_seq in inits:
            if GLOBAL_DEADLINE - time.monotonic() < 80.0:
                break
            a, sc = _cur_optimize(init_seq, general_specs)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                _consider(a, f"general N={a.size}")

    # Final fallback if nothing else worked.
    if best_a is None or best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = np.array([2.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0])
        best_score = _fast_score_seq(best_a)

    best_a = np.clip(np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0).ravel()
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        best_a = best_a / s
    else:
        best_a = np.array([2.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0])

    # Verify with the official evaluator and choose the safer candidate if needed.
    try:
        official = _official_score(best_a)
    except Exception:
        official = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(official) or official > max(best_score, 1.77777777777):
        # Small positive fallback with known score 16/9.
        fb = np.array([2.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0])
        try:
            fb_score = _official_score(fb)
        except Exception:
            fb_score = 16.0 / 9.0
        if np.isfinite(fb_score) and (not np.isfinite(official) or fb_score < official):
            best_a = fb
            best_score = fb_score

    print(f"[final] fast={best_score:.10f} official={official if np.isfinite(official) else 'n/a'} len={best_a.size}")
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
